**18/05/2022 15:03** little tip i would have for you when it comes to drawing shit loads of OBs and levels on the chart

only keep the closest stuff to price

delete the rest

so instead of this [image]

messy as mf

you only keep the relevant stuff [image]

it cant go to those lower ones without hitting that closest one first

so thats the one most relevant to price

ofc it doesnt hurt to have them all drawn out

youre not doing anything wrong by having them on there

but just for keeping shit neat

this is what i do

i find it actually helps my analysis too, only keeping the most essential things on the chart keeps me focused on the important aspects of a trade

**10/08/2022 19:04** [image]

[image]

rebranding a lot of my market structre names, as ive refined them to having different meanings

rBMS no longer exists, as i've combined it with sBMS to create keyBMS

cBMS is the same, as always

and tBMS has just had a cheeky rebrand to intermediateBMS, because that name suits it better. considering every single MSB on the planet is 'technically a break in market structure'. i didnt like how you could say "well a cBMS is also technically a BMS". so iBMS it is.

and then some complexities that i've ironed out in terms of intermediate levels.

we already had icBMS, we just called it tcBMS. same thing. just with the name change.

however ikBMS is different. before i used to operate under the assumption that rBMS was rBMS. nothing more. however i am coming to understand that rBMS (or rather kBMS) has to be used within the context of the entire picture, and that means keeping the "real" key highs and lows in mind when looking at the most recent kBMS too.

if you care about trading whatsoever, then spend 4 minutes, out of the 60 years you have left to live, reading that ^^^

**09/03/2023 10:01** [image]

so in terms of this, a trade is still valid even if it goes 2r before entry

litz has a rule where if it goes 5r+ without giving him an entry, only then will he cancel his order

me and CT dont have that same rule, its valid till stoploss or tp for us

­
first example: price hits 3R without giving entry = still valid to sell the BZ if it goes back up

second example: price hits 3R after giving entry = not valid to sell the BZ if it goes back up, because the liquidity in the BZ has been used already. [image]

**17/03/2023 08:26** GU is called cable because GBPUSD was one of the first ever trading pairs on forex

but back then, we had no high speed internet

so how did we get the price of USD, and tell it to the people in england

we transmitted the price through a fat undersea CABLE that went all the way under the ocean from the USA to the UK

by the time Euro was established, we had much faster methods of sending information. so we would send price quotes through FIBRE optics

UCAD is LOONIE because canada's $1 coin has a picture of a LOON on it, a type of bird. but like i said, loonie sounds dumb as shit and ucad is so much better [image]

swissy is unimaginative, just because its swiss franc

NZDUSD is kiwi for obvious reasons

AUDUSD is aussie for obvious reasons

and there are more dumb ass names for cross pairs, like EG is chunnel - cause of the channel tunnel that connects uk and france

but nobody cares about those

**21/03/2023 16:44** the trader was mentoring some guy, who would always do this kinda thing. and he'd say "i dont really like this", or "i like this trade a lot" - and he would change his leverage based off how much he 'liked' the trade

and the trader said to him

what the fuck does you liking it or not have to do with anything

what are your stats on this setup

what concrete statistics do you have on this trade, that make you 'not like it'

and ofc the guy had none

you need to either stay consistent, and only ever use BZ, or only ever use RC, no mix and matching

or you need stats that show "when BZ is over XX, then use RC (as long as RC is under YY)"

like how you dropped to 2.2r consistently

instead of fumbling around and sometimes doing 3, sometimes doing 2 (edited)

just depending on gut feeling

youre consistent now

and thats good

consistency in your system, means your backtesting will be consistent

if your backtesting is consistent, then your results will be consistent

if your results are consistent, then your trades will be consistent

just stick to BZ from now on

**31/08/2023 10:31** Really liked what RB put in CE so im gonna post it in here for everyone

📍1. Master 1 setup

Mastering 1 setup is much better than trading 10 setups
90% of the time, more trades == more gain

Practice. Memorize. Be the best in that setup.

📍2. Execute

Focus 100% on your setup, ignore all the noise
Think in probabilities to improve your execution
Decrease your risk to reduce influences on your emotions

Set rules. Trade. Win/Lose. Repeat

📍3. Don't stop

You don't become a successful trader in 30 days
You don't gain experience and knowledge in 30 days
Stop thinking in days, start thinking in years

Be consistent. Learn from your mistakes. Never give up.

📍4. Shape your mindset

If you want discipline, follow your rules
If you want confidence, reduce your risk
If you want patience, meditate
If you want consistency, keep showing up
If you want persistence, never give up

Shape your mindset, shape your trading.

📍5. Leverage your trading skills

Discipline will make you win
Confidence will make you win bigger
Consistency will make you win the game

Trust the process. Trading is a marathon, not a sprint.

📍6. Plan your success

Create 30-60-90 day plans:

Follow rules
Review your trades
Learn from your mistakes
Increase your monthly/quarterly goals

Never stop improving.

📍7. Improve 10x your trading

Focus 100% on 1 setup
Never stop trading
Develop trading skills
Plan your trading success

Stop wasting time. Start becoming a successful trader.

**03/01/2024 08:11** yeah too much leverage again

you were trying to buy $355k with a maximum of the 30x leverage offered by ftmo [image]

355k / 30x lev, you would need at least $11,857 in your account to open this trade

that 'units' thing at the bottom, has to be less than 30x smaller than your account balance

otherwise the trade wont open

$9500 account * 30x leverage, the maximum amount of units you can buy is 285,000

so you would have to just use smaller lots until that 'units' number is below 285k

**15/10/2024 13:37** you can take single taps

only 1 tap

so now thats 2 confluences for the short

**21/10/2024 22:57** also a reminder when there are multiple possible setups that you would need to check the correlations

for example AUDUSD + NZDUSD are 82.7% correlated with one another [image]

which means if you get stopped out on one, theres an 82.7% chance youre getting stopped out on the other one too

so basically shorting both would be double risk

BUT

EJ is only 6.7% correlated to aussie, and only 25% correlated to kiwi [image]

so taking an EJ trade along with either of these two would be perfectly fine

**25/10/2024 00:34** i normally do it in reverse

before bed, i'll check the nearest swing points that we could conceivably hit the next day, to see if theyre big enough

if they are, i mark them with a box and change the line to - - - - - -

[image]

and then i just set an alert on it

so i can ignore that chart for the whole day

if it gets tagged, i set an alarm to go off 5 mins before the close, so i can see if its closing as a sweep or an msb

and then i have plenty of time to work out my SL

and calculate the lots

see which highs or lows are nearby

measure them to see if theyre significant

**21/03/2025 18:49** Is there any chance you set alerts for certain significant highs if they get swepped?

**21/03/2025 19:47** yep

just draw a ray on all the significant highs and then set an alert on it [image]

but keep in mind the ATR is always changing

lets say the daily ATR is 60.3 pips, and you find a high thats 59.2 pips

well that high is not significant is it

.....today

maybe in 2 months, when we come up and sweep that high, the ATR might be 54 pips

and now that swing high (59.2 pips) IS larger than the current daily ATR (54 pips)

so what i personally do is i draw those rays and set alerts on every swing high thats 'close enough'

and then if i get an alert in a month, i go peep if the high is significant or not (based on todays ATR). and if its not significant, sweet, i just delete the line and move on with my day

**25/03/2025 10:54** yeah ive noticed that a lot

its rare for them not to be

**25/03/2025 10:55** Does the size of the candle usually equal to the ATR?

**25/03/2025 10:56** DAILY atr

its how much price moves in 1 DAY

(on avg)

how much does price also move in 1 day?

a daily candle

the daily atr is just the avg size of the last months daily candles

=======================================
okay so 2 scenarios:
1). we're sweeping an old (significant) high, and a new tiny high.
2). we're sweeping an old (significant) high, and a new (reasonably sized) high. (edited) [image]

because neither of the green highs are significant, i dont give a single flying fuck if they get swept

so im only focusing on the (significant) red highs to be swept

however

[image]

we only trade a sweep if there are no excess touches to the level

remember how a level gets weaker and weaker the more it gets touched

touch #1 - the swing high is formed
touch #2 - we sweep and short
good trade ✅

vs

touch #1 - the swing high is formed
touch #2 - the resistance gets weaker
touch #3 - we sweep and resistance gets weaker again
bad trade ❌

so even though im not looking at those insignificant green highs to get swept... i am focusing on if theyre making the resistance weak

cause i want strong resistance if im shorting

i want that shit to TANK
